- `hmh07-lua-follow4:010` (original lines 111-111, 111-111, 111-111): Builds the tabbed scoreboard header with team/enemy backings, faction colors/icons, names, game timer and scores. Each nonnil model callback updates its own visual field. FFAType and Codcaster are stored state predicates; factions.isCoDCaster delivery requests state validation. Creates self via LUI.UIElement.new, BlurBG via LUI.UIImage.new, TintBot via LUI.UIImage.new, TintBody via LUI.UIImage.new, TintTop via LUI.UIImage.new, EnemyBacking via CoD.TabbedScoreboardHeaderEnemy.new, TeamBacking via CoD.TabbedScoreboardHeaderTeam.new, YourTeamTopBar via CoD.TabbedScoreboardHeaderTopbar.new, EnemyTeamTopBar via CoD.TabbedScoreboardHeaderTopbar02.new, EnemyTeamLine via LUI.UIImage.new, YourTeamLine via LUI.UIImage.new, GameTimer00 via LUI.UIText.new, EnemyIcon via LUI.UIImage.new, CodcasterTeam2Logo via LUI.UIImage.new, YourTeamIcon via LUI.UIImage.new, CodcasterTeam1Logo via LUI.UIImage.new, VS via LUI.UIText.new, GameTypeText via LUI.UIText.new, TimeText via LUI.UIText.new, TabbedScoreboardHeaderScores via CoD.TabbedScoreboardHeaderScores.new, GameType via CoD.TabbedScoreboardHeaderTitle.new, CodCasterTeam1Name via LUI.UIText.new, CodCasterTeam2Name via LUI.UIText.new. Stores state predicates FFAType, Codcaster; their body effects require state-system evaluation.

- `hmh07-lua-follow4:137` (original lines 206-206, 176-176, 179-179, 206-206, 176-176, 179-179, 206-206, 176-176, 179-179): Missing clientNum model returns. Creates visibility/text prompt models. A nonnegative other-client number gets safe clientNum/isBot values, visibility from nonnil clientNum and not isBot, and muted/unmuted text chosen through the engine mute query. Self/invalid selection hides visibility and writes default mute text. It does not mute a player. Gets the controller model. If currentFocusedClient is absent, requests client model into an unused local and creates currentFocusedClient without assigning that local. Registers a callback setting the footer model to delivered model:get(). Do not invent an initialization write. Asserts Tabs exists then calls SetFocusToFirstSelectableItem on it. Failed assertion prevents the helper call. Only on PC with an occludedBy chain, walks to the last menu then calls GoBackToMenu with root/controller/original menuName. No cycle detection is present.
